================ QinQ TEST (TX context descriptor with outer + inner tag, ETH_MACVIR.CSVL, ETH_MACIVIR, IVTIR) ================
  frame sent: 60 bytes, EtherType 0x88B5 at bytes 12..13. With a tag the MAC puts type + tag there and
  shifts the rest by 4 bytes per tag. RDES3.LT: 1 = type, 4 = one VLAN tag, 5 = two VLAN tags
  RDES0 = outer tag [15:0] (inner tag [31:16] only with stripping); ctx TDES3: bit 31 OWN and bit 23 CDE must be 0
  Each case: description, setup, expectation -> [FLOW lines] -> result line -> frame sent / received split
  at DA / SA / tag(s) / type / payload.
  case A prints the [10]/[11] FLOW lines (context descriptor + frame). D, E send a frame that already
  carries the two tags 88 A8 07 D0 81 00 E0 C8 (68 bytes) and let the context change the inner one.

  --- case A: MACIVIR VLP = 1, VLC = insert: the register adds the inner tag to every frame. Context outer S 07D0 + inner C 0064, VTIR = insert
ctx: 2 (0 none, 1 one tag, 2 outer + inner)
      setup : context descriptor: outer TDES3.VT = 0x07D0, inner TDES2.IVT = 0x0064, IVTIR = none; frame: 60 bytes, TDES2.VTIR = insert
      expect: 68 bytes, bytes 12..19 = 88 A8 07 D0 81 00 00 64, then the EtherType 88 B5
[10] tx_desc[0] = context descriptor QinQ: TDES2=0x00640000 (IVT=0x0064) TDES3=0xC00307D0 (VT=0x07D0, IVTIR=0)

  compares them with ETH_MACVTR. expect/actual: PASS = a frame came back, DROP = none within 5000 us.
